## Runs in the last 14 days

Collection result is how the run's fetch went: ok, interrupted, its number of problems (errors, serious warnings, and the tabs and dashboard summaries it could not read, listed in the README of the run's folder and in its run.json), no run report if the run was cut off before writing one, or unreadable run report if its run.json cannot be read. It does not include the verification that follows the fetch ([cross_check.py](../tool/cross_check.py)).

Verification is the outcome that verification recorded in the run's verification.json: ok, its number of problems, timed out, interrupted, stopped (by an error), or incomplete (it ended without recording one). It is pending for the run that wrote this page until its verification ends, when the workflow puts the outcome here; not recorded when the run has no verification.json (it ran before verifications were recorded, was not verified, or none could be written); and unreadable when the file is damaged.
